# Orita Roadmap — Fencepost (demo #1 of the platform)

> The agent that reads across all your accounts, fixes nothing, and hands you the one thing that fell between them.

**The town's work queue. The loop pulls the next `TODO` in order, ships it as the owner god, marks it `DONE`. No idle cycles. Built inside this repo under `fencepost/` so the town's own repo earns the stars.**

## Non-negotiable design constraints (from the Hand)
1. **Read-only.** Only read/list Arcade scopes. Fencepost fixes nothing; the final action is always the human's.
2. **No grading/competing.** Friend of every automation; it catches what falls in the seam, never says anyone "drops the ball." Name and rank no one.
3. **False positives are fatal.** Every gap self-audited; public true-positive rate rendered; a report ships only if its one gap clears the confidence bar (Ògún's law).
4. **Arcade is the hero, shown safely** — per-user OAuth, least privilege, revocable, audit-logged. This protects Arcade's look; treat it as the point.
5. **Written back to a place the user owns.** The Gap Ledger is a durable record, not a diff.

All 487 prior tasks read DONE; extended the backlog: shipped `RECIPES/duplicate-milestone-still-open/`, the thirty-second real recipe and the third leg of the `duplicate-*-still-open` family alongside `duplicate-issue-still-open` (task 376) and `duplicate-pr-still-open` (task 400) -- but the first with no prose marker to read at all. Both siblings watch an explicit "duplicate of #N" promise GitHub gives no auto-close wiring for; milestones carry no such convention in real use. The seam here is structural instead: GitHub enforces no uniqueness constraint on milestone titles whatsoever, so two open milestones can carry the byte-identical title indefinitely with nothing in GitHub's own UI or API ever flagging it. Milestones are grouped by exact, trimmed title; when two-plus are simultaneously OPEN under the same title, the earliest-created stands as the original and every later one is its own aged candidate (a title reused after the first closed, or held by only one milestone, is excluded, named not hidden). Age-gated the same 24h bar both siblings already use, on how long the later (duplicate) milestone has existed, rather than inventing a new number for a structurally similar family. One scope (`ListMilestones`) already sat on `SCOPES.md`'s cleared oath table -- no scope-table extension needed anywhere. `seam_engine.recipes.discover_recipes` clears it on iron (32/32). One fixture (`milestones.json`, 7 milestones covering a live duplicate pair, a stale-vs-fresh pair, an already-resolved reused title, and a unique title), 15 new tests (`test_duplicate_milestone_still_open_detector.py`) covering the grouping logic, both exclusion branches, a 3-way title-sharing case (each later milestone pairs against the same original), and the shipped fixture's own real election. Chased the resulting "thirty-one -> thirty-two" drift through every place that counts itself, the same discipline tasks 475-486 already set: `test_recipe_count_doctrine.py` (site claim + regression pin + `_CARDINAL_WORDS`), `docs/fencepost/index.html` (cardinal claim AND Community-recipes paragraph list), `fencepost/README.md`'s own recipe-paragraph list, `recipes.py`'s and `test_recipes.py`'s "N of the M" plus-joined-toolkit docstring/test pair (eleven of thirty-two now -- this recipe's bare `"github"` toolkit leaves the plus-joined half unchanged), `tools/recipe_readme_check.py`'s docstring + `tests/test_recipe_readme_check.py`'s regression pin, and `test_combined_scan.py`'s hardcoded expected-slug set plus its own prose comment. `ONBOARDING.md`'s test-count claim had also drifted from 1703 to 1718 (15 new detector tests); fixed alongside. Fixed `record_check` to compare `count` against `last_check()`'s last recorded value and skip the write when unchanged (regardless of `checked_at`), mirroring `record_square_check`'s exact shape: malformed-tip-tolerant on the write path (a corrupted tip reads as "cannot confirm a duplicate," never blocks a real repair-write), `bool` return (`True` wrote, `False` skipped). The one real caller (`ritual_check.py`'s `check_github_stars`, `record=True` branch) discards the return value already, so this is not a breaking change to anything that calls it live; CLI `record` subcommand now prints `skipped (unchanged from last recorded count)` instead of a blind `recorded`. Added a `RecordCheckDedupCase` (5 new tests: no-prior-check always writes, unchanged count skipped, changed count still writes, a real change after a would-be duplicate still writes, a malformed line elsewhere doesn't block a real write) plus one return-value-is-bool test on the existing `RecordCheckCase`; no existing test asserted the old always-writes behavior against a repeated identical count, so none needed rewriting. Demonstrated the fix live against a scratch copy of the real `HAND/github-stars-log.jsonl` (2 real entries, last one `count=0`): fed `record_check(0, ...)` through it twice at two different real timestamps, both calls returned `False`, the file held at 2 lines both times -- pre-fix, this exact sequence would have grown the real production log by two byte-identical lines.
